      @pytest.mark.asyncio
      async def test_chat_completions_non_streaming(self, client, api_key_headers, test_model):
          """测试非流式聊天补全"""
          from app.gateway.adapter import MessageRole

          # Mock the adapter
          mock_message = Mock()
          mock_message.role = MessageRole.ASSISTANT
          mock_message.content = "Hello! How can I help you today?"
          mock_message.tool_calls = None

          mock_choice = Mock()
          mock_choice.index = 0
          mock_choice.message = mock_message
          mock_choice.finish_reason = "stop"

          mock_response = Mock()
          mock_response.choices = [mock_choice]
          mock_response.usage = {"prompt_tokens": 10, "completion_tokens": 20, "total_tokens": 30}

          with patch("app.api.chat.create_adapter") as MockAdapter:
              mock_adapter = Mock()
              mock_adapter.chat_completion = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_response)
              MockAdapter.return_value = mock_adapter

              # Mock billing service to avoid DB issues
              with patch("app.api.chat.BillingService") as MockBillingService:
                  mock_billing = Mock()
                  mock_billing.check_quota = Mock(return_value=(True, None))
                  mock_billing.record_usage = Mock()
                  MockBillingService.return_value = mock_billing

                  response = client.post(
                      "/api/v1/chat/chat/completions",
                      headers=api_key_headers,
                      json={
                          "model": test_model.model_code,
                          "messages": [
                              {"role": "user", "content": "Hello!"}
                          ],
                          "temperature": 0.7,
                          "max_tokens": 100,
                          "stream": False
                      }
                  )

              assert response.status_code == 200
              data = response.json()
              assert data["object"] == "chat.completion"
              assert data["model"] == test_model.model_code
              assert "choices" in data
              assert len(data["choices"]) > 0
              assert "usage" in data
              assert "openllm_meta" in data

      @pytest.mark.asyncio
      async def test_chat_completions_no_quota(self, client, api_key_headers, test_model):
          """测试配额不足的情况"""
          # Mock billing service to return no quota
          with patch("app.api.chat.BillingService") as MockBillingService:
              mock_service = Mock()
              mock_service.check_quota = Mock(return_value=(False, None))
              MockBillingService.return_value = mock_service

              response = client.post(
                  "/api/v1/chat/chat/completions",
                  headers=api_key_headers,
                  json={
                      "model": test_model.model_code,
                      "messages": [
                          {"role": "user", "content": "Hello!"}
                      ]
                  }
              )

      async def test_api_key_channel_carries_external_identity(
          self, db_session, test_api_key
      ):
          """API Key 通道 + 可信源 + 身份头 → GatewayIdentity.external_* 非空"""
          from app.api.openllm_gateway import get_gateway_identity
          from app.core.config import settings

          api_key_obj, full_key = test_api_key
          request = _make_gateway_request({
              "Authorization": f"Bearer {full_key}",
              "X-Proxy-Source": "openbase-llm-proxy",
              "X-User-ID": "ext-user-001",
              "X-Org-ID": "ext-org-001",
          })
          with patch.object(settings, "TRUSTED_PROXY_SOURCES", "openbase-llm-proxy"):
              identity = await get_gateway_identity(request, db_session)
          assert identity is not None
          assert identity.auth_method == "api_key"
          assert identity.external_user_id == "ext-user-001"
          assert identity.external_org_id == "ext-org-001"

      async def test_request_context_propagates_external_identity(self, db_session, test_user):
          """_build_request_context 将 external_user_id/external_org_id 注入 RequestContext"""
          from app.api.openllm_gateway import GatewayIdentity, _build_request_context

          user = test_user
          identity = GatewayIdentity(
              user=user,
              user_id=str(user.id),
              auth_method="api_key",
              external_user_id="ext-user-001",
              external_org_id="ext-org-001",
          )
          context = _build_request_context(identity, "req-ext-1")
          assert context.external_user_id == "ext-user-001"
          assert context.external_org_id == "ext-org-001"

          plain_identity = GatewayIdentity(user=user, user_id=str(user.id))
          plain_context = _build_request_context(plain_identity, "req-plain-1")
          assert plain_context.external_user_id is None
          assert plain_context.external_org_id is None
